| T384 | The winning collector formally apologizes for his followers, then gratitude is routed to God instead of Serena while pious bowing becomes more harassment. |
| T385 | Threatening the supposedly hostile cat turns into delighted petting and a bargain to delay departure; claws provoke divine-skin warning and comic territorial dispute over bath/soap god categories. |
| T386 | Urgent search versus civic duty becomes low-level self-devaluation and worry about looking like a deserter. |
| T387 | Useful confirmation of Aqua’s reunion comes with the priestess’s market-loss envy and failed seductive glance at the rich swordsman. |
| T388 | Vanir greets the horror as a cuddly hell pet, gets his hand bitten off, switches from pet discipline to destruction beam and reconstructs the limb while calling it an ill-trained stray. |
| T389 | Vanir laughs at the erased find-me request and attention-seeking trip; worried Wiz realizes her earlier requested death concerned the barrier. |
| T390 | Vanir offers a loving handsome demon with not enough head; hopeful Wiz imagines poor arithmetic and learns the skull is physically absent, then remembers demons lack sex. |
| T391 | Village hagiography/accident-identification exchange resolves with Zesta272. Actual whereabouts question273 starts next pursuit-proximity/teasing premise. |
| T392 | Warm farewell promises to guard Aqua’s home coexist with Kazuma’s terror of more leveling and Vanir’s instruction to return alive so he can transport the remaining loot. |
| T393 | Warm homecoming and an emotional armor gift embarrass Kazuma because it was an unusable leftover; black-armored Darkness sounds like a villain’s guard, then Megumin asks for her missing gift. |
| T394 | While the hero refuses to wait for Kazuma, Aqua’s attempt to defend him yields an extensive defect list; she only remembers his dependable side after the hero leaves. |
| T395 | Wiz answers worsening combat by raising the slain dragon and cheerfully proposing a whole dungeon takeover in her shop apron; praise makes the apologetic corpse-user bashful and eager. |
| T396 | Wiz counterthreatens to keep secretly acquired stock but immediately tries to save her precious goods from confiscation. |
| T397 | Wiz is relieved no child was experimented on yet now dislikes demons; Vanir protests judging his entire race from one specimen. |
| T398 | Yunyun question162 begins recollected church-business bridge with no actual explanation yet. |
| T399 | Yunyun’s familiar anxious restraint reanimates Aqua’s water-expert pride; Aqua then retreats from alligators, falls into the reservoir and accidentally purifies it while everyone debates rescue and she calls for absent Kazuma. |
| T400 | Zero-talent explanation ends418. Move explicit otherworld-reveal stimulus419 into its own immediately following disclosure premise, preserving I original’s overlapping419 context. |
| T401 | Zesta calls a divine epiphany upon realizing a novel use of the reflection curse; a coercive assault threat attracts competing devotees and collapses Serena’s information refusal into a desperate plea. |
| T402 | Zesta self-buffs and lays claim to Serena, prompting accusations of abusing priestly office as rivals brawl; Kazuma quietly offers conditional protection for information. |
| T403 | Zesta wants to join the exciting castle assault until Kazuma’s exact teleport capacity leaves him as the stranded ninth person; he instantly elects to go home. |
